# Commands · C · part 8

1,584 entries, chatbi-maintain-knowledge to check-deps. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **chatbi-maintain-knowledge**: Route a governed knowledge-reference maintenance request - lint an existing or candidate reference against DOC-002/003, retrieve route-ready references, and record an atomic change (co-located with the Warehouse model). Never publish a reference with open lint issues; never approve a canonical metric definition (human owner only, SEM-003). ([lostinheaven-knt/chatbi-harness](https://github.com/lostinheaven-knt/chatbi-harness/blob/HEAD/harness/.claude/commands/chatbi-maintain-knowledge.md))
- **chatbi-maintain-model**: Route a governed model/semantic maintenance request - generate a change-impact manifest (DOC-004), propose candidate code/metadata/reference/eval changes, and gate delivery on full sync (model-metadata-semantic-reference-Skill-tests-downstream-eval). A model-only change without sync is blocked by the Stop gate; protected actions require human owner approval (SEM-003). Never self-certify; never bypass the Cycle 3 analysis loop. ([lostinheaven-knt/chatbi-harness](https://github.com/lostinheaven-knt/chatbi-harness/blob/HEAD/harness/.claude/commands/chatbi-maintain-model.md))

- **che-eject**: Eject Che safely and reversibly: uninstall multi-agent adapters, move whitelist files to trash, clean up .gitignore snippets from client projects, and allow restore. 4 subcommands: plan [flags] | trash-list | restore TRASH_SLUG [flags]. 3 mandatory safety gates for destructive operations. NEVER uses rm — only moves to .trash/che-eject/. Absolute blacklist (never touched): user_rules/, bindings/registry.jsonl, memory/, .git/, node_modules/. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-eject.md))

- **che-figma**: Design V4 with explicit preference for Figma backend when available in runtime. Alias of /che-design — same 4-mode pipeline: Social, UI-UX, Design System, OR Logo & Branding (SVG + brandbook). ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-figma.md))
- **che-fix**: Bug fix che. Scientific debug loop: Hypothesize → Instrument → Reproduce → Analyze → Fix → Verify. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-fix.md))
- **che-graph**: Graph & Knowledge wrapper around graphify CLI (pipx graphifyy package, local AST only — no network). 4 canonical subcommands: refresh | query &lt;NL question&gt; | path &lt;A&gt; &lt;B&gt; | stats. Always idempotently adds graphify-out/ to .gitignore. Falls back to grep-based heuristics if graphify CLI not installed. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-graph.md))
- **che-import**: Import project durable data from a .tar.gz archive. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-import.md))
- **che-knowledge**: Create and evolve the project's durable knowledge base: glossary.md (vocabulary) plus &lt;domain&gt;/conventions.md (observed practice). Previewed as a dry-run diff, gated by keep/alter/revert before every write. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-knowledge.md))
- **che-manual-plan**: Creates or updates the manual test plan as an EXECUTABLE contract: `che manual-plan sync` scaffolds or migrates it (idempotent), the skill fills each acceptance criterion's yaml block from the SPEC / ticket / diff, and `che manual-plan validate` is the gate. Use before /che-manual-test (which executes it) or /che-manual-verification (which renders it for a layperson). ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-manual-plan.md))
- **che-manual-test**: Execute a validated manual test plan step-by-step with the Chrome DevTools MCP (UI) and the terminal (HTTP/API), capturing screenshot/video/console/network evidence per acceptance criterion. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-manual-test.md))
- **che-manual-verification**: Writes a plain-language, step-by-step guide so a NON-TECHNICAL person can verify the increment by hand: what was implemented/changed/fixed, how to test it locally or on the PR preview, and — when neither is possible — the exact route to a deployed environment. Captures the evidence to collect (logs, screenshots, video). Writes the guide; does NOT execute it (that is /che-manual-test) and gives no verdict (that is /che-review). ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-manual-verification.md))
- **che-merge**: Resolve Git merge conflicts with MINIMUM BLAST RADIUS, one file at a time. DEFAULT favors current worktree (ours). 3 cases: trivial auto-resolve (whitespace/newline/auto-merge), clash asks side by side, ambiguity NEVER resolved alone → asks between 2 max options + justification. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-merge.md))
- **che-onboarding**: Interactive: capture or read the durable Product Context + Roadmap for a project. Product Context = 8 mandatory sections (pitch, 3 personas, domain keywords, integrations, 5-10 hard invariants, compliance risks, auth roles matrix, ref URLs). Roadmap = 4 blocks (confirmed/planned/backlog/OUT-OF-SCOPE). Stored in .registry/projects/&lt;slug&gt;/ alongside xray output. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-onboarding.md))
- **che-parallel**: Explicit parallel execution mode. ERROR if not parallelizable (no serial fallback — parallel-or-bust). ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-parallel.md))
- **che-pr-comments**: Triage ALL PR comments: classify human/bot, actionable vs nit, produce implementation plan + polite reply drafts. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-pr-comments.md))
- **che-project**: Manage flat Che projects (&lt;CHE_WORKSPACES_ROOT&gt;/&lt;slug&gt;/). 5 subcommands: init|create|add REPO_PATH --slug SLUG [--domain D] [--name N] | list | remove SLUG [--dry-run|--no-dry-run --confirm] | restore TRASH_SLUG | trash-list. Scaffolds 8 domain folders + worktrees/ + _db/ + roles/ + the durable docs. Requires an explicit slug and a git path. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-project.md))
- **che-query**: Run parameterized SQL against the Che state store (che_state.sqlite). Restricted to SELECT/EXPLAIN/PRAGMA by default. Use --force explicitly for writes. Automatically invokes rebuild-index if the DB doesn't exist yet. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-query.md))
- **che-rag**: RAG (Retrieval Augmented Generation) search with hybrid BM25 lexical + vector embeddings. Builds incremental index from project durable docs (architecture, specs, envelopes, decisions). sqlite-vec optional, zero-deps fallback always works. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-rag.md))
- **che-refactor**: Behaviour-preserving refactoring (Fowler). Applies established refactorings (extract function, inline, extract variable, replace conditional with polymorphism, split phase, ...) to improve readability, maintainability, scalability and testability WITHOUT changing observable behaviour. PRECONDITION: a green test safety net; missing coverage means characterization tests first. Never runs inside /che-ship. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-refactor.md))
- **che-review**: High-impact BLOCKING-only code review on a GitHub PR. Runtime/Security/Deps/SCope only — never style bikeshed. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-review.md))
- **che-sanitize**: Sanitize the Che state store: purge old decisions (default &gt; 180 days), trim decisions count to a max cap (default 5000 most recent), remove RELEASED/old bindings (&gt; max-age), remove tasks marked DONE older than max-age. Always supports --dry-run to preview the impact before running. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-sanitize.md))
- **che-scope-check**: 4-checks scope audit from PRD/ticket/task-graph against GitHub PR or local worktree. Checks: 1) full scope delivery (ACs mapped), 2) unit/e2e tests cover expected behavior, 3) required docs updated (AGENTS/README/runbooks), 4) new env vars declared in infra/env parsers. Returns verdict GREEN/YELLOW/RED. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-scope-check.md))
- **che-search**: Full-text semantic search across the Che project knowledge base using SQLite FTS5 BM25 scoring. Covers: tasks + envelopes body, decisions payload, specs documents. Optional scope filter and top-K limit. Falls back to LIKE-based search gracefully if the FTS5 virtual tables could not be created. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-search.md))
- **che-ship**: End-of-task ship: atomic conventional commits → push → DRAFT PR → assign user. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-ship.md))
- **che-simplify**: Produces a SIMPLIFICATION PLAN for something already planned or implemented (epic, task graph, gh-stack, module, area). Applies the Simplicity Bias with the GOALS held fixed: classifies essential vs accidental complexity, then proposes the smallest plan that still meets the goals, with a decision trail. Output is a spec-shaped plan. NOT a verdict (/che-review) and NOT an explanation (/che-explain). ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-simplify.md))
- **che-skip**: Override a quality/compliance gate. Logs skip with reason. EXTREME CAUTION — especially compliance-heavy. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-skip.md))
- **che-spec**: Generate or update a Che Execution Specification (SPEC). Standalone, or called by /che-act. Accepts 4 inputs: existing spec, ticket URL, legacy-project PRD .md path, inline description. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-spec.md))
- **che-status**: Print concise CURRENT che session status from task_graph.md. No skill invocation needed. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-status.md))
- **che-summary**: Generate an immediate interim session summary (even if not all tasks are done). ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-summary.md))
- **che-task**: Cross-domain task picker, resumer and status manager for the Kahn Wave task graph. 5 subcommands: list | show TID | resume TID | set-status TID STATUS | graph-summary. Binds ACTIVE_DOMAIN + ACTIVE_TASK_ID automatically and recommends the correct domain-specialized slash command. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-task.md))
- **che-ui-testing**: Helper skill ui-testing-contracts: delivers RTL Priority Order (Kent Dodds) snippets + Playwright byTestId Flockr boilerplate + 3-part data-testid convention regex lint (G8.3) + Category 8 UI Hygiene cross-reference (code-review gate ONDA1 + SbE Selector Contract ONDA2). ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-ui-testing.md))
- **che-worktree**: Bind a git checkout to a project: &lt;CHE_WORKSPACES_ROOT&gt;/&lt;project&gt;/worktrees/&lt;name&gt;/. 4 subcommands: add REPO_PATH --project SLUG --name NAME [--force] | list --project SLUG | show PROJECT NAME | remove PROJECT NAME [--dry-run|--no-dry-run --confirm]. A worktree is the ONLY Che concept that binds a filesystem path; it is shared by every session and is never created per session. ([laionazeredo/che-ai](https://github.com/laionazeredo/che-ai/blob/HEAD/commands/che-worktree.md))

- **check**: `bca check` compares metrics against thresholds at each metric's own scope (function, file, or container) and exits non-zero when any value exceeds its limit. It is the CI integration point: wire it into a build step and a regression in code complexity fails the pipeline before the change lands.
